It controls epistemic status and containment, never evidence visibility or presumptive acceptance/rejection.","legacy_docket_assessment":{"adjudicable":false,"packet":null,"status":"invalid"},"packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity":[{"focus_ref":"87:1","identity_status":"legacy_unbound","packet_identity":null,"protocol":"focus-trace-hermetic-response-v4","reader_id":"reader_hft_a","trace_kind":"reconstructed"}]},"reader_synthesis_count":11,"source_present":true,"structured_insight_count":15,"unstructured_record_count":0},"identity":{"ayah_ref":"87:1","lane":"global","linguistic_source_ref":"87:1","surface_ref":"87:1","unit_kind":"numbered_ayah"},"primary_floor":{"source_ref":"87:1","target_tokens":[["En",["87:1:4"]],["yüce",["87:1:4"]],["Rabbinin",["87:1:3"]],["adını",["87:1:2"]],["yücelt",["87:1:1"]]],"text":"En yüce Rabbinin adını yücelt."},"schema_version":"commentary-v5-hermetic-scope-packet-v1","scope":{"hft":{"assigned_record_count":11,"authoring_policy":{"every_assigned_record_requires_explicit_review":true,"parseable_hft_is_always_visible_to_authoring":true,"provenance_affects_qualification_not_visibility":true,"scope_mismatch_affects_lane_assignment_not_visibility":true,"unresolved_branch_citations_do_not_suppress_records":true},"authoring_status":"visible_with_provenance_qualification"},"lane_contract":"reader walks and cross-run publications with explicit trust labels","pericope":{"ayah_from":1,"ayah_to":19,"id":"s087-p01-001-019","label":"Whole surah","number":1,"refs":["87:1","87:2","87:3","87:4","87:5","87:6","87:7","87:8","87:9","87:10","87:11","87:12","87:13","87:14","87:15","87:16","87:17","87:18","87:19"]},"readiness":{"authoring_effect":"Docket readiness is reported for source provenance. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0c63050a6df73241200c","text":"1. **Praise as purification of the raised name.** Lexical evidence: سبح B002 purification/declaring free; سمو B005 name as raised designation and B001 rising high; ربب B001 lordship/authority; علو B001 rising/height and B002 high rank. Structural cue: the ayah compresses an imperative, a name, lordship, and superlative elevation. Assigned role: سبح clears the named Lord from lower associations. Abductive move: the packet gives purification and elevation; I infer that the act aims to keep the \"name\" aligned with الأعلى. Reading change: \"glorify\" reads as maintaining the name's altitude, not only praising verbally.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B002","root_000666/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_11acff14f69aaa125a57","text":"The sharpest later correction came from 87:5: once pasture becomes dark refuse, `س ب ح/B002` reads as keeping the Highest free from both flourishing and decay, not praising only through attractive products. The linked verbs in 87:2–5 also made the otherwise dormant coursing image of `س ب ح/B004` structurally visible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_37130fe50d90512e64fd","text":"87:16-17 made the opening more sharply vertical than expected: \"Most High\" is not merely a title but a criterion for judging the nearer/lower life against the later/enduring one. 87:18-19 also retrospectively made \"name\" feel like a written-and-recited marker carried through earlier sheets.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B007","root_001042/B002","root_001042/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_37aa600c4e5ab2497bd2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:1:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B007\"]],[\"87:1:4\",\"root_001042\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"En Yüce nitelemesi, yakın hayatı seçen insanın sahte yükselme arzusuna karşı gerçek üstünlüğü belirler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_59cf46189c4e9948fb8c","text":"1. **Elevation by distancing from decay.** Lexical evidence: س ب ح B002 (purification/declaring free), س م و B005 (name as raised designation), ر ب ب B001/B002 (lordship plus nurture), ع ل و B001/B002 (height and rank). Structural cue: the opening command is followed by created/proportioned/guided growth in 87:2-4 and then blackened refuse in 87:5. Assigned role: tasbih separates the Lord's raised name from the cycle of pasture emergence and collapse. Abductive move supplied by me: if all generated forms pass through greening and withering, the divine name must be read as not one item inside that ecology. Reading change: \"Exalt\" becomes an act of ontological clearing, not only verbal praise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_606a9da6c1e2bd6f9288","text":"4. **Elevation also activates a danger of rival elevation.** Lexical evidence: علو B003 haughty domination, B004 mastery; سمو B007 rivaling by vying upward; later كبر B006 self-magnification and شقو B002 hardship in 87:11-12. Structural cue: the superlative الأعلى stands before later \"كبرى\" fire and worldly preference. Assigned role: rightful elevation contrasts with human false elevation. Abductive move: the packet supplies both noble and blameworthy elevation branches; I infer contrast because the surah later divides the fearful rememberer from the avoidant wretched one. Reading change: the command is also anti-idolatrous in posture: raise the Lord's name so rival claims of height collapse.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_000666/B001","root_000745/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_727b09517af95996d3be","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:1:1\",\"root_000666\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:1:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B005\"]],[\"87:1:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu buyruk, 87:15'te adın anılması ve namazla insan davranışına dönüşen programın başlangıcıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001042/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_75c2aca17419cff2deb9","text":"3. **Ascending name against lower preference.** Lexical evidence: ع ل و B005 (upper side), د ن و B002/B003 in 87:16 (nearer/lower worldly side), ء خ ر B001/B002 in 87:17 (later/postponed), ب ق ي B001 (remaining). Structural cue: الأعلى at the opening is mirrored by الدنيا/الآخرة at the end. Assigned role: \"Most High\" sets the vertical scale by which the later-but-enduring is not lower. Abductive move: spatial high/low imagery can structure temporal preference. Reading change: 87:1 pre-reads the ethical contrast of 87:16-17.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_000745/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_89ebe6ae5f57ad51e9de","text":"2. **“Lord” is activated as a process of nurture.** `ر ب ب/B002` gives repair, completion, and stage-by-stage nurture; `س م و/B005` makes `ٱسْم` a raised designation, and `ع ل و/B002` gives high rank. The tightly chained verbs in 87:2–4—create, proportion, measure, guide, bring out—supply the stages through which this lordship operates. Their role is to unpack `رَبِّكَ` functionally, while the name and superlative rank that process above its products. I infer that the relative clauses explain the title. The phrase changes from bare possession or authority into attentive administration toward completion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_89f460ebdbee9374220f","text":"1. **Tasbih performs conceptual separation.** `س ب ح/B002` is purification and declaring free, while 87:2–5 runs from creation and proportion to pasture and its conversion into refuse. This complete material cycle is the structural cue: the highest Lord is to be kept distinct from every generated state, including flourishing and decay. The branch assigns `سَبِّحِ` the role of separating source from transient products. I infer that the following cycle supplies what the command must not confuse with the Lord. The imperative therefore reads as disciplined differentiation, not only praise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ca6f0d783c5c329ff8b7","text":"87:15 confirmed the ritual-practice reading by repeating \"ذكر اسم ربه فصلى.\" 87:16-17 sharpened the elevation contrast: الدنيا as nearer/lower activates \"الأعلى\" as a spatial and value reversal, not simply an epithet.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B004","root_000745/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cf836d48cbd533425c9d","text":"3. **Praise acquires a coursing motion.** Alongside worship, `س ب ح/B004` supplies swimming or coursing motion. The uninterrupted sequence of result-linked verbs in 87:2–5, together with `س م و/B001` and `ع ل و/B001` as rising, activates a moving rather than static image. This branch makes the command traverse the whole ordered process while maintaining an upward orientation. The directional continuity is an abductive overlay; no literal medium for swimming is stated. The reading becomes dynamic praise that courses through the signs of formation and transformation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d764a12129de37e72df1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:1:1\",\"root_000666\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yüceltme, Rabbi büyüme ve çürüme gibi yaratılmış durumlarla karıştırmaktan arındıran bir ayırma eylemine dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_da0ed5cec96827142e2d","text":"2. **Tasbih as ritual-prayer bridge to 87:15.** Lexical evidence: سبح B001 worship through praise/prayer; ذكر B003/B004 in 87:15; صلو B003 prescribed worship and B002 supplication. Structural cue: 87:1 opens with an imperative to sanctify the name; 87:15 describes the successful one remembering the Lord's name then praying. Assigned role: 87:1 is the command-form of the practice later embodied by the purified person. Abductive move: the packet supplies the repeated \"name + Lord\" and prayer branch; I infer ritual realization from the repetition. Reading change: the ayah becomes a program that later becomes a human habit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e6129721a5b2c46d1922","text":"3. **The \"Lord\" as nurturer of formation, not only sovereign above.** Lexical evidence: ربب B002 repair/nurture/completion; خلق B002 originating and سوي B002 sound completion in 87:2; قدر B005 deliberate arrangement and هدي B001 guidance in 87:3. Structural cue: the relative clauses after 87:1 immediately define the Lord by making, proportioning, measuring, and guiding. Assigned role: رب becomes the caretaker who completes what He originates. Abductive move: the packet supplies sovereignty and nurture branches; I infer that the following clauses specify which branch is activated. Reading change: \"your Lord, the Highest\" is a high nurturer whose elevation is visible through precise care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f58b562362ac2d1c9b90","text":"2. **Name as stable marker in a transmission chain.** Lexical evidence: س م و B008 (good repute spread), ذ ك ر B004/B007/B009 in 87:9, 87:15, ق ر ء B001/B002 in 87:6, ص ح ف B002/B003 in 87:18-19. Structural cue: name appears at the start and again in 87:15 before written former sheets close the surah. Assigned role: the name is the raised designation around which recitation, reminder, and scripture cohere. Abductive move: repeated oral and written media preserve the name by circulating it. Reading change: the command inaugurates a public-readable chain of praise rather than a private utterance alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000666/B004","root_000745/B001","root_001042/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f7316bc67e7742f94fec","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:1:1\",\"root_000666\",[\"B004\"]],[\"87:1:2\",\"root_000745\",[\"B001\"]],[\"87:1:4\",\"root_001042\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yüceltme, oluşum ve dönüşüm işaretleri boyunca yukarı yönlü bir akış gibi de okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f745793365563807a6ff","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"87:1:3\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rab sözü, sonraki yaratma, ölçme, yöneltme ve besleme adımlarıyla aşama aşama tamamlayan bakım anlamı kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
